Purification in 92:18 was the greater surprise, because it made `ء و ل:B002` productive retrospectively—the significance of a beginning can be read from what its course becomes.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_535b2d384cad37c7bb37","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:13:3\",\"root_000019\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sonranın erteleme boyutu, geciken sonucun sahiplik alanından çıkmadığını, yalnızca ortaya çıkış zamanının ötelenmiş olduğunu gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000067/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_569b07d0d48fe24c4472","text":"3. **Beginning read from its outcome.** The same root inventory gives `ء و ل:B002` as return to outcome or final meaning. Placed beside `ٱلْءَاخِرَة`, this dormant branch lets the “first” be reread through what a course eventually becomes, especially with the opposed formations of 92:16–18. The branch relation is lexical, but applying outcome back onto the fixed superlative noun is exploratory and abductive. The first is no longer a disposable prelude; it is an origin whose significance appears in its later result.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001","root_000067/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8842afc5aed0bab4f646","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:13:3\",\"root_000019\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:13:4\",\"root_000067\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Son ile ilkin tek sahiplik altında birlikte anılması, başlangıç ve sonuç arasındaki bütün güzergâhı aynı egemenliğin içine alır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000067/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a8cbd2697fd23ee82bc7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:13:4\",\"root_000067\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İlkin sonuçtan okunabilme boyutu, bir başlangıcın anlamının daha sonra dönüştüğü şeyle açığa çıkmasına imkân verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000067/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_da4c4279ba399bc233a9","text":"3. **Return to outcome and interpretation.** Evidence: `ء و ل` B002 (return to final meaning/outcome), activated by the whole surah’s paired beginnings and endings. Structural cue: earlier cosmic oaths receive moral “ta’wil” in the later human scene. Mechanism: the first signs return to their outcome in guidance, fire, and satisfaction. Abductive move: I infer that ownership of الأولى includes ownership of its interpretation. Reading change: beginnings are read by their mآl, their final return.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e69111b07d63647aa1b6","text":"The later `ع س ر` B006 difficult-birth branch in 92:10 changed this ayah most: creation’s male/female pair became a generative order that can be either opened by giving or blocked by withholding. 92:18’s `ز ك و` growth then made creation’s fertility morally active.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ea0d174695bbf628cac0","text":"1. **A single ownership spanning opposed endpoints.** `ٱلْءَاخِرَة` anchors `ء خ ر:B001` (what is later), and `ٱلْأُولَىٰ` anchors `ء و ل:B001` (beginning and precedence). Their marked reverse order—later before first—follows guidance in 92:12 and precedes warning and divergent outcomes: falling in 92:11 remains behind, while avoidance and purification appear in 92:17–18. The packet supplies endpoint terms and outcome sequence; the inference is that ownership encloses the whole trajectory between them. The ayah reads as control across temporal extent, not as two separately possessed domains.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f658b4e43b3f7fded6a3","text":"1. **First and last under one ownership.** Evidence: `ء خ ر` B001 (later/other after first), `ء و ل` B001 (beginning/precedence). Structural cue: order is reversed in the phrase—last named before first. Mechanism: divine possession encloses both outcome and origin, destabilizing self-sufficiency in between. Abductive move: I infer that reversal makes the end govern the reading of the beginning. Reading change: the ayah turns time itself into a held domain, not two separate worlds.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
